<h1 class="hero-overlay__title"><span>Welcome to the Barbican</span>
</h1>
<div class="hero-overlay__description">
<div><p>A world-class arts and learning centre, the Barbican pushes the boundaries of all major art forms including dance, film, music, theatre and visual arts. </p>
</div>

<div class="external-content__body">
<h2 class="discover-title">
<a href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/read-watch-listen/life-rewired-shorts-better-by-emily-downe" tabindex="-1">
<span>Life Rewired Shorts - Better by Emily Downe</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="external-content__copy wysiwyg-content">
<div>
<div class="micro-copy">
<p>What if everything was the same - but it looked better? <strong>Emily Downe</strong> presents her animated short, 'Better', exploring the ideal worlds of a perfectionist culture where a fantasy jungle acts as a portal to bring these worlds into 'real life'. </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="discover-title">
<a href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/read-watch-listen/life-rewired-shorts-extra-life-by-matthew-harmer" tabindex="-1">
<span>Life Rewired Shorts - Extra Life by Matthew Harmer</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="external-content__copy wysiwyg-content">
<div>
<div class="micro-copy">

</div>

<span>Life Rewired Shorts - Meet Meat by Helen Plumb</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="external-content__copy wysiwyg-content">
<div>
<div class="micro-copy">
<p>Is meat grown in a lab vegetarian? 'Meet Meat' puts the idea of meat under the microscope to hear both sides of the argument as we look ahead to the future of food production. </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="external-content__button">

<h2 class="discover-title">
<a href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/read-watch-listen/life-rewired-shorts-divided-we-scroll-by-klaas-diersmann" tabindex="-1">
<span>Life Rewired Shorts - Divided We Scroll by Klaas Diersmann</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="external-content__copy wysiwyg-content">
<div>
<div class="micro-copy">
<p>How long have you spent scrolling today? Klaas Diersmann presents an experimental and eerie depiction of our intimate yet divisive and compulsive relationships with mobile technologies.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="external-content__button">

<h2 class="discover-title">
<a href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/read-watch-listen/life-rewired-shorts-wifi-in-the-glen" tabindex="-1">
<span>Life Rewired Shorts - WiFi in the Glen</span>
</a>
</h2>
<div class="external-content__copy wysiwyg-content">
<div>
<div class="micro-copy">
<p>How much do you rely on technology? Meet the residents of a remote community in the heart of the Scottish Highlands in ‘WiFi in the Glen’ to learn some of the surprising ways technology has influenced this ancient place.  </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="external-content__button">
<a class="button button--branded button--inline" href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/read-watch-listen/life-rewired-shorts-wifi-in-the-glen" tabindex="-1">

<div><p><strong>Nearby public transport</strong><br/>
We are within walking distance from a number of London Underground stations, the closest being Barbican, St Paul’s and Moorgate. The nearest train stations are Liverpool Street and Farringdon. Bus Route 153 runs directly past the Barbican along Chiswell Street. Plan your journey and find more information about getting to the Barbican on our '<a href="https://www.barbican.org.uk/your-visit/getting-here/map-directions">Your Visit</a>' pages.</p>
<p>We have free bicycle spaces and paid <a href="/your-visit/getting-here/car-parking">car parking spaces</a> available</p>
